class FormularySelectionStore {
    values = new Map();
    get(date) { return this.values.get(date) || 0; }
    set(date, index) { this.values.set(date, Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(index) || 0))); }
    clear(date) { if (date)
        this.values.delete(date);
    else
        this.values.clear(); }
    clamp(date, count) {
        if (count <= 0)
            return 0;
        const value = Math.max(0, Math.min(this.get(date), count - 1));
        if (value !== this.get(date))
            this.values.set(date, value);
        return value;
    }
}

exports.ProperResolver = ProperResolver;
function cleanLines(lines, language) {
    const out = [];
    for (let line of lines || []) {
        line = String(line).trim();
        if (!line)
            continue;
        if (/^\(sed .*rubrica (1570|1910|1930|divino afflatu)/i.test(line))
            continue;
        if (line === "&Gloria") {
            out.push(GLORIA[language]);
            continue;
        }
        if (line === "$Per Dominum") {
            out.push(PER_DOM[language]);
            continue;
        }
        if (line === "$Deo gratias") {
            out.push(language === "fr" ? "℟. Deo gratias — Rendons grâce à Dieu." : "℟. Deo gratias.");
            continue;
        }
        if (/^!/.test(line)) {
            out.push(line.slice(1).trim());
            continue;
        }
        line = line.replace(/^[Vv]\.\s*/, "℣. ").replace(/^[Rr]\.\s*/, "℟. ").replace(/\+\+/g, "✠").replace(/✠|✚|✙|✛|✜|✝|✞/g, "✠").replace(/(^|\s)\+(?=($|\s|[,.\;:!?)]))/g, "$1✠");
        out.push(line);
    }
    return out.join("\n").trim();
}
function textFrom(sources, section) {
    return {
        lat: cleanLines(sources.la.map.get(section) || [], "la"),
        en: cleanLines(sources.en.map.get(section) || [], "en"),
        fr: cleanLines(sources.fr.map.get(section) || [], "fr"),
    };
}
function firstExisting(sources, ids) {
    return ids.find(id => sources.la.map.has(id) || sources.en.map.has(id) || sources.fr.map.has(id)) || null;
}
function numbered(sources, base, includeBase = false) {
    const ids = new Set();
    for (const language of ["la", "en", "fr"]) {
        for (const id of sources[language].order) {
            if ((includeBase && id === base) || new RegExp(`^${base}\\d+$`).test(id))
                ids.add(id);
        }
    }
    if (includeBase && (sources.la.map.has(base) || sources.en.map.has(base) || sources.fr.map.has(base)))
        ids.add(base);
    return [...ids].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, undefined, { numeric: true })).map(id => textFrom(sources, id)).filter(value => value.lat || value.en || value.fr);
}
function ruleInfo(sources) {
    const text = (sources.la.map.get("Rule") || []).join("\n");
    const match = text.match(/Prefatio=([^\s;]+)/i);
    return { raw: text, gloria: /^Gloria$/m.test(text), credo: /^Credo$/m.test(text), preface: match ? match[1] : "Communis" };
}

function normalizeProper(meta, sources, preface, diagnostic) {
    const rules = ruleInfo(sources);
    const normal = new Set(["Officium", "Rank", "Rule", "Introitus", "Oratio", "Lectio", "Graduale", "GradualeP", "Tractus", "Sequentia", "Evangelium", "Offertorium", "Secreta", "Communio", "Postcommunio"]);
    const preparatoryLessons = [];
    for (let n = 1; n <= 12; n += 1) {
        const lessonId = `LectioL${n}`;
        if (!(sources.la.map.has(lessonId) || sources.en.map.has(lessonId) || sources.fr.map.has(lessonId)))
            continue;

function cloneSources(sources) {

    }

}

function cleanLines(lines, language) {
    const out = [];
    for (let line of lines || []) {
        line = String(line).trim();
        if (!line)
            continue;
        if (/^\(sed .*rubrica (1570|1910|1930|divino afflatu)/i.test(line))
            continue;
        if (line === "&Gloria") {
            out.push(GLORIA[language]);
            continue;
        }
        if (line === "$Per Dominum") {
            out.push(PER_DOM[language]);
            continue;
        }
        if (line === "$Deo gratias") {
            out.push(language === "fr" ? "℟. Deo gratias — Rendons grâce à Dieu." : "℟. Deo gratias.");
            continue;
        }
        if (/^!/.test(line)) {
            out.push(line.slice(1).trim());
            continue;
        }
        line = line.replace(/^[Vv]\.\s*/, "℣. ").replace(/^[Rr]\.\s*/, "℟. ").replace(/\+\+/g, "✠").replace(/✠|✚|✙|✛|✜|✝|✞/g, "✠").replace(/(^|\s)\+(?=($|\s|[,.\;:!?)]))/g, "$1✠");
        out.push(line);
    }
    return out.join("\n").trim();
}

}

function numbered(sources, base, includeBase = false) {
    const ids = new Set();
    for (const language of ["la", "en", "fr"]) {
        for (const id of sources[language].order) {

                ids.add(id);
        }
    }
    if (includeBase && (sources.la.map.has(base) || sources.en.map.has(base) || sources.fr.map.has(base)))
        ids.add(base);
    return [...ids].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, undefined, { numeric: true })).map(id => textFrom(sources, id)).filter(value => value.lat || value.en || value.fr);
}

function renderRichText(value, language, context = 'default') {

        const label = mark || (section.role && context !== 'choir' ? roleLabel(section.role, language) : '');
        return `<div class="rolePassage${classes ? ' ' + classes : ''}">${label ? `<span class="roleLabel">${esc(label)}</span>` : ''}<div class="roleBody">${renderParagraphBody(section.text, context)}</div></div>`;
    }).join('');
}
function swapLabels(block, language) {
    const vern = language === 'fr' ? 'français' : 'English';
    const reveal = block.primaryLanguage === 'lat' ? (language === 'fr' ? 'Toucher pour le français' : 'Tap for English') : (language === 'fr' ? 'Toucher pour le latin' : 'Tap for Latin');
    const release = block.primaryLanguage === 'lat' ? (language === 'fr' ? 'Toucher pour le latin' : 'Tap for Latin') : (language === 'fr' ? `Toucher pour le ${vern}` : 'Tap for English');
    const back = block.primaryLanguage === 'lat' ? (language === 'fr' ? 'Retour au latin' : 'Back to Latin') : (language === 'fr' ? `Retour au ${vern}` : 'Back to English');
    return { reveal, release, back };
}

function renderSwapReading(block, language, primaryRenderer = null, secondaryRenderer = null) {
    const labels = swapLabels(block, language);
    const context = block.role ? 'choir' : 'default';
    const primaryHtml = primaryRenderer ? primaryRenderer(block.primary) : renderRichText(block.primary, language, context);

    if (block.role && block.compactContinuation) {
        const continuation = language === 'fr' ? 'continue' : 'continues';

}

            continuation: true,
        };
        out.choir = [...(out.choir || []), dynamicEvent];

        case "preGospelChants": return (proper.preGospelChants || []).some(ch => hasText((0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(ch?.text))) || hasText((0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.gradual));
        case "sequence": return hasText((0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.sequence));
        case "gospel": return hasText((0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.gospel));
        case "offertory": return hasText((0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.offertory));
        case "secrets": return normaliseList(proper.secrets).length > 0;
        case "preface": return hasText((0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.preface));
        case "communion": return hasText((0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.communion));
        case "postcommunions": return normaliseList(proper.postcommunions).length > 0;
        case "superPopulum": return hasText((0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.superPopulum));
        default: return false;
    }
}
function attachProper(step, proper, properAvailable) {
    const out = clone(step);
    if (out.id === "gospel-announcement") {
        out.text = gospelAnnouncementText(proper);
        if (Array.isArray(out.gestures) && out.gestures[0]) {
            out.gestures[0].anchorLat = 'Sequéntia ✠ sancti Evangélii';
            out.gestures[0].anchorEng = 'Continuation ✠ of the Holy Gospel';
        }
    }
    if (out.properSlot) {
        const sourceReady = properAvailable && properSlotHasSource(proper, out.properSlot);
        const resolved = sourceReady ? properText(proper, out.properSlot, out.id) : undefined;
        out.properStatus = hasText(resolved) ? "ready" : "unavailable";
        if (resolved)
            out.text = resolved;
        else
            delete out.text;
        if (out.id === "secret")
            out.audibleFragment = secretConclusion;
    }
    if (out.concurrentProperChoir) {
        const choirSourceReady = properAvailable && properSlotHasSource(proper, out.concurrentProperChoir);
        const choirText = choirSourceReady ? properChoirText(proper, out.concurrentProperChoir, out.id) : undefined;
        const dynamicEvent = {
            id: `${out.id}-${out.concurrentProperChoir}-choir`,
            role: "choir",
            properSlot: out.concurrentProperChoir,
            properStatus: hasText(choirText) ? "ready" : "unavailable",
            ...(choirText ? { text: choirText } : {}),
            continuation: true,
        };
        out.choir = [...(out.choir || []), dynamicEvent];
    }
    delete out.concurrentProperChoir;
    return out;
}

class MemoryTextFetcher {
    fetchImpl;
    timeoutMs;
    memory = new Map();
    constructor(fetchImpl, timeoutMs = 12000) {
        this.fetchImpl = fetchImpl;
        this.timeoutMs = timeoutMs;
    }
    async get(url, diagnostic) {
        const cached = this.memory.get(url);
        if (cached !== undefined) {
            diagnostic?.cacheHits.push({ url, layer: "memory" });
            return cached;
        }
        diagnostic?.requestedFiles.push(url);
        const timeout = new Promise((_, reject) => setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(`Source request timed out · ${url}`)), this.timeoutMs));
        const request = this.fetchImpl(url, { cache: "no-cache", redirect: "follow" }).then(async (response) => {
            if (!response.ok) {
                const error = new Error(`HTTP ${response.status} · ${url}`);
                error.status = response.status;
                throw error;
            }
            return response.text();
        });
        const text = await Promise.race([request, timeout]);
        this.memory.set(url, text);
        return text;

function attachProper(step, proper, properAvailable) {
    const out = clone(step);
    if (out.id === "gospel-announcement") {
        out.text = gospelAnnouncementText(proper);
        if (Array.isArray(out.gestures) && out.gestures[0]) {
            out.gestures[0].anchorLat = 'Sequéntia ✠ sancti Evangélii';
            out.gestures[0].anchorEng = 'Continuation ✠ of the Holy Gospel';
        }
    }
    if (out.properSlot) {
        const sourceReady = properAvailable && properSlotHasSource(proper, out.properSlot);
        const resolved = sourceReady ? properText(proper, out.properSlot, out.id) : undefined;
        out.properStatus = hasText(resolved) ? "ready" : "unavailable";
        if (resolved)
            out.text = resolved;
        else
            delete out.text;
        if (out.id === "secret")
            out.audibleFragment = secretConclusion;
    }
    if (out.concurrentProperChoir) {
        const choirSourceReady = properAvailable && properSlotHasSource(proper, out.concurrentProperChoir);
        const choirText = choirSourceReady ? properChoirText(proper, out.concurrentProperChoir, out.id) : undefined;
        const dynamicEvent = {
            id: `${out.id}-${out.concurrentProperChoir}-choir`,
            role: "choir",
            properSlot: out.concurrentProperChoir,
            properStatus: hasText(choirText) ? "ready" : "unavailable",
            ...(choirText ? { text: choirText } : {}),
            continuation: true,
        };
        out.choir = [...(out.choir || []), dynamicEvent];
    }
    delete out.concurrentProperChoir;
    return out;
}

function properChoirText(proper, slot, stepId) {
    if (!proper)
        return undefined;
    if (slot === "preGospelChants") {
        const chants = (proper.preGospelChants || []);
        if (!chants.length)
            return (0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.gradual);
        if (stepId === "gospel-prep")
            return (0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(chants[chants.length - 1]?.text);
        return properText(proper, slot, stepId);
    }
    if (slot === "offertory")
        return (0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.offertory);
    return properText(proper, slot, stepId);
}
function properSlotHasSource(proper, slot) {
    if (!proper)
        return false;
    switch (slot) {
        case "introit": return hasText((0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.introit));
        case "collects": return normaliseList(proper.collects).length > 0;
        case "epistle": return hasText((0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.epistle));
        case "preGospelChants": return (proper.preGospelChants || []).some(ch => hasText((0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(ch?.text))) || hasText((0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.gradual));
        case "sequence": return hasText((0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.sequence));
        case "gospel": return hasText((0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.gospel));
        case "offertory": return hasText((0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.offertory));
        case "secrets": return normaliseList(proper.secrets).length > 0;
        case "preface": return hasText((0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.preface));
        case "communion": return hasText((0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.communion));
        case "postcommunions": return normaliseList(proper.postcommunions).length > 0;
        case "superPopulum": return hasText((0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.superPopulum));
        default: return false;
    }
}
function attachProper(step, proper, properAvailable) {
    const out = clone(step);
    if (out.id === "gospel-announcement") {
        out.text = gospelAnnouncementText(proper);
        if (Array.isArray(out.gestures) && out.gestures[0]) {
            out.gestures[0].anchorLat = 'Sequéntia ✠ sancti Evangélii';
            out.gestures[0].anchorEng = 'Continuation ✠ of the Holy Gospel';
        }
    }
    if (out.properSlot) {
        const sourceReady = properAvailable && properSlotHasSource(proper, out.properSlot);
        const resolved = sourceReady ? properText(proper, out.properSlot, out.id) : undefined;
        out.properStatus = hasText(resolved) ? "ready" : "unavailable";
        if (resolved)
            out.text = resolved;
        else
            delete out.text;
        if (out.id === "secret")
            out.audibleFragment = secretConclusion;
    }
    if (out.concurrentProperChoir) {
        const choirSourceReady = properAvailable && properSlotHasSource(proper, out.concurrentProperChoir);
        const choirText = choirSourceReady ? properChoirText(proper, out.concurrentProperChoir, out.id) : undefined;
        const dynamicEvent = {
            id: `${out.id}-${out.concurrentProperChoir}-choir`,
            role: "choir",
            properSlot: out.concurrentProperChoir,
            properStatus: hasText(choirText) ? "ready" : "unavailable",
            ...(choirText ? { text: choirText } : {}),
            continuation: true,
        };
        out.choir = [...(out.choir || []), dynamicEvent];
    }
    delete out.concurrentProperChoir;
    return out;
}

function filterOrdinarySteps(steps, options, proper) {
    let out = steps;
    if (proper?.hasGloria === false)
        out = out.filter(step => step.id !== "gloria");
    if (proper?.hasCredo === false)
        out = out.filter(step => step.id !== "credo");
    if (options.form === "sung" && options.incenseSung === false) {
        out = out.filter(step => step.id !== "incense-entrance" && step.id !== "incense-off");
    }
    if (options.omitJudica || proper?.isRequiem)
        out = out.filter(step => step.id !== "judica");
    if (options.faithfulCommunion === false) {
        out = out.filter(step => !["second-confiteor", "ecce", "domine-non-sum-dignus", "communion-faithful"].includes(step.id));
    }
    else if (options.secondConfiteor) {
        const idx = out.findIndex(step => step.id === "ecce");
        if (idx >= 0)
            out = [...out.slice(0, idx), makeSecondConfiteor(!!options.joinConfiteor), ...out.slice(idx)];
    }
    // 1962 SOT: at Sung Mass, if the faithful receive, the Communion chant begins
    // with distribution after the communicants' triple Domine non sum dignus.
    // If nobody receives, it begins during the priest's Communion.
    if (options.form === "sung" && options.faithfulCommunion !== false) {
        out = out.map(step => {
            if (step.id !== "priest-communion" || !step.concurrentProperChoir)
                return step;
            const copy = { ...step };
            delete copy.concurrentProperChoir;
            return copy;
        });
    }
    if (proper?.omitLastGospel || proper?.followedByLiturgicalAction) {
        out = out.filter(step => step.id !== "last-gospel-announcement" && step.id !== "last-gospel");
    }
    return out;
}
function insertSuperPopulum(steps, proper, properAvailable) {
    const text = properAvailable ? (0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper?.superPopulum) : undefined;
    if (!text)
        return steps;
    const idx = steps.findIndex(step => step.id === "postcommunion");
    if (idx < 0)
        return steps;
    const step = {
        id: "super-populum",
        phase: "Conclusion",
        section: "Postcommunion",
        title: { lat: "Oratio super populum", en: "Prayer over the People", fr: "Oraison sur le peuple" },
        actor: "celebrant",
        audibility: "audible",
        privateAction: false,
        text,
        properSlot: "superPopulum",
        properStatus: "ready",
    };
    return [...steps.slice(0, idx + 1), step, ...steps.slice(idx + 1)];
}

/* mass-sot-field-cues-20260927-v2 */
/* mass-sot-final-field-safe-20260927-v3 */
function insertSermonPause(steps) {
    const idx = steps.findIndex(step => step.id === "gospel");
    if (idx < 0 || steps.some(step => step.id === "sermon-pause"))
        return steps;
    const sermon = {
        id: "sermon-pause",
        phase: "Mass of the Catechumens",
        section: "Gospel & Creed",
        title: { en: "Sermon / Homily · if present", fr: "Sermon / Homélie · si présent" },
        subtitle: { en: "If preaching occurs, stay here until it ends. If not, continue.", fr: "S’il y a une prédication, restez ici jusqu’à sa fin. Sinon, continuez." },
        actor: "preacher",
        priestPosition: "Preaching place",
        audibility: "audible",
        privateAction: false,
        posture: { value: "CUSTOM", policy: "local", source: "sermon" },
        gestures: [],
        explanation: "The sermon is a navigation pause, not a prayer-text step of the Ordo Missae. Do not advance the Mass timeline because of elapsed preaching time."
    };
    return [...steps.slice(0, idx + 1), sermon, ...steps.slice(idx + 1)];
}
function buildMassSequence(options) {
    const proper = options.proper || null;
    const properAvailable = options.properAvailable !== false && !!proper;
    let steps = clone(options.form === "sung" ? sungTemplate : lowTemplate);
    steps = filterOrdinarySteps(steps, options, proper);
    steps = steps.map(step => attachProper(step, proper, properAvailable));
    steps = insertSuperPopulum(steps, proper, properAvailable);
    if (!options.exceptionalProfile)
        steps = insertSermonPause(steps);
    if (options.exceptionalProfile)
        steps = (0, exceptional_days_1.applyExc

function easterVigil(steps) {
    const donor = donorGroup('easter_vigil');
    const lead = donor.filter(step => ['vigil-fire', 'vigil-candle', 'vigil-lumen', 'vigil-exsultet', 'vigil-prophecies'].includes(step.id));
    const lauds = donor.find(step => step.id === 'vigil-lauds');
    let mass = trimBefore(steps, ['incense-entrance', 'kyrie']);
    mass = mass.filter(step => !['introit', 'agnus', 'peace-prayer', 'communion-proper', 'last-gospel-announcement', 'last-gospel'].includes(step.id));
    const abl = mass.findIndex(step => step.id === 'ablutions');
    if (lauds && abl >= 0)
        mass = [...mass.slice(0, abl + 1), lauds, ...mass.slice(abl + 1)];
    return [...lead, ...clone(vigilBridge), ...mass];
}
function holyThursday(steps) {
    let out = steps.filter(step => !['judica', 'credo', 'peace-prayer', 'blessing', 'last-gospel-announcement', 'last-gospel'].includes(step.id));
    const dismissal = out.find(step => step.id === 'dismissal');
    if (dismissal) {
        dismissal.title = { lat: 'Benedicámus Dómino', en: 'Benedicamus Domino', fr: 'Benedicamus Domino' };
        dismissal.text = clone(benedicamus);
        dismissal.exceptional = { rite: 'holy-thursday-1962', phase: 'Mass ending', liturgicalColour: 'White' };
    }
    const placeat = out.findIndex(step => step.id === 'placeat');
    const tail = donorGroup('holy_thursday');
    if (placeat >= 0 && tail.length)
        out = [...out.slice(0, placeat + 1), ...tail, ...out.slice(placeat + 1)];
    return out;
}
function requiem(steps, form) {
    let out = steps.filter(step => !['incense-entrance', 'peace-prayer', 'blessing'].includes(step.id));
    const prep = out.find(step => step.id === 'gospel-prep');
    if (prep)
        prep.id = 'gospel-prep-requiem';
    if (form === 'sung') {
        const gospel = out.find(step => step.id === 'gospel');
        if (gospel) {
            gospel.text = stripLausTibi(gospel.text);
            gospel.variantNoteId = 'requiem-sung-no-laus-tibi';
        }
    }
    return out;
}
function applyExceptionalProfile(stepsInput, profileId, form, properInput) {
    const steps = clone(stepsInput);
    const proper = properInput;
    switch (profileId) {
        case 'candlemas-1962': return [...donorGroup('candlemas'), ...trimBefore(steps, ['incense-entrance', 'introit'])];
        case 'ash-wednesday-1962': return [...donorGroup('ash_wednesday'), ...trimBefore(steps, ['incense-entrance', 'introit'])];
        case 'palm-sunday-1962': return [...donorGroup('palm_sunday'), ...trimBefore(steps, ['incense-entrance', 'introit']).filter(step => !['last-gospel-announcement', 'last-gospel'].includes(step.id))];
        case 'holy-thursday-1962': return holyThursday(steps);
        case 'good-friday-1962': return goodFriday(proper);
        case 'easter-vigil-1962': return easterVigil(steps);
        case 'requiem-1962': return requiem(steps, form);
        case 'ember-saturday-1962':
        case 'extended-readings-mass-1962': return insertPreparatoryLessons(steps, proper);
    }
}

À la fin, les chantres commencent solennellement Kyrie eleison ; la Messe commence.' }, exceptional: { rite: 'easter-vigil-1962', phase: 'Litany II', liturgicalColour: 'White' } },
];
function easterVigil(steps) {
    const donor = donorGroup('easter_vigil');
    const lead = donor.filter(step => ['vigil-fire', 'vigil-candle', 'vigil-lumen', 'vigil-exsultet', 'vigil-prophecies'].includes(step.id));
    const lauds = donor.find(step => step.id === 'vigil-lauds');
    let mass = trimBefore(steps, ['incense-entrance', 'kyrie']);
    mass = mass.filter(step => !['introit', 'agnus', 'peace-prayer', 'communion-proper', 'last-gospel-announcement', 'last-gospel'].includes(step.id));
    const abl = mass.findIndex(step => step.id === 'ablutions');
    if (lauds && abl >= 0)
        mass = [...mass.slice(0, abl + 1), lauds, ...mass.slice(abl + 1)];
    return [...lead, ...clone(vigilBridge), ...mass];
}
function holyThursday(steps) {
    let out = steps.filter(step => !['judica', 'credo', 'peace-prayer', 'blessing', 'last-gospel-announcement', 'last-gospel'].includes(step.id));
    const dismissal = out.find(step => step.id === 'dismissal');
    if (dismissal) {
        dismissal.title = { lat: 'Benedicámus Dómino', en: 'Benedicamus Domino', fr: 'Benedicamus Domino' };
        dismissal.text = clone(benedicamus);
        dismissal.exceptional = { rite: 'holy-thursday-1962', phase: 'Mass ending', liturgicalColour: 'White' };
    }
    const placeat = out.findIndex(step => step.id === 'placeat');
    const tail = donorGroup('holy_thursday');
    if (placeat >= 0 && tail.length)
        out = [...out.slice(0, placeat + 1), ...tail, ...out.slice(placeat + 1)];
    return out;
}
function requiem(steps, form) {
    let out = steps.filter(step => !['incense-entrance', 'peace-prayer', 'blessing'].includes(step.id));
    const prep = out.find(step => step.id === 'gospel-prep');
    if (prep)
        prep.id = 'gospel-prep-requiem';
    if (form === 'sung') {
        const gospel = out.find(step => step.id === 'gospel');
        if (gospel) {
            gospel.text = stripLausTibi(gospel.text);
            gospel.variantNoteId = 'requiem-sung-no-laus-tibi';
        }
    }
    return out;
}
function applyExceptionalProfile(stepsInput, profileId, form, properInput) {
    const steps = clone(stepsInput);
    const proper = properInput;
    switch (profileId) {
        case 'candlemas-1962': return [...donorGroup('candlemas'), ...trimBefore(steps, ['incense-entrance', 'introit'])];
        case 'ash-wednesday-1962': return [...donorGroup('ash_wednesday'), ...trimBefore(steps, ['incense-entrance', 'introit'])];
        case 'palm-sunday-1962': return [...donorGroup('palm_sunday'), ...trimBefore(steps, ['incense-entrance', 'introit']).filter(step => !['last-gospel-announcement', 'last-gospel'].includes(step.id))];
        case 'holy-thursday-1962': return holyThursday(steps);
        case 'good-friday-1962': return goodFriday(proper);
        case 'easter-vigil-1962': return easterVigil(steps);
        case 'requiem-1962': return requiem(steps, form);
        case 'ember-saturday-1962':
        case 'extended-readings-mass-1962': return insertPreparatoryLessons(steps, proper);
    }
}

    const legacyAntiphonal = /(^|\n)\s*[SP]\.\s/m.test(lat) && /(^|\n)\s*M\.\s/m.test(lat);

}

function choirEvents(step) {
    return (step.choir || []).map(event => {
        if (event.properStatus === "unavailable" || !event.text)
            return unavailable(step, String(event.properSlot || "proper"), "choir");
        return {
            stepId: step.id,
            kind: "choir",
            actor: "choir",
            text: event.text,
            properSlot: event.properSlot,
            continuation: !!event.continuation,
        };
    });
}
function projectStep(step, mode) {
    const events = [];
    const choir = choirEvents(step);
    if (mode === "missal") {
        if (step.properStatus === "unavailable" && step.properSlot)
            events.push(unavailable(step, step.properSlot, step.actor));
        else if (step.text)
            events.push({ stepId: step.id, kind: "altar", actor: step.actor, text: step.text, properSlot: step.properSlot });
        events.push(...choir);
        return { mode, stepId: step.id, events };
    }
    // VOX: choir is always part of what is actually heard.
    events.push(...choir);
    if (step.properStatus === "unavailable" && step.properSlot && step.audibility !== "quiet" && step.audibility !== "silent") {
        events.push(unavailable(step, step.properSlot, step.actor));
        return { mode, stepId: step.id, events };
    }
    if (step.audibility === "audible") {
        if (step.text)
            events.push({ stepId: step.id, kind: "altar", actor: step.actor, text: step.text, properSlot: step.properSlot });
    }
    else if (step.audibility === "mixed") {
        if (step.audibleFragment)
            events.push({ stepId: step.id, kind: "altar", actor: step.actor, text: step.audibleFragment, properSlot: step.properSlot });
        else if (step.text && !step.privateAction)
            events.push({ stepId: step.id, kind: "altar", actor: step.actor, text: step.text, properSlot: step.properSlot });
    }
    else if (step.audibility === "choir") {
        // Choir already emitted above.
    }
    return { mode, stepId: step.id, events };
}
function projectSequence(steps, mode) {
    return steps.map(step => projectStep(step, mode));
}

const VALID_AUDIBILITY = new Set(["audible", "quiet", "silent", "mixed", "choir"]);
function validateText3(value, path, issues) {
    if (!value)
        return;
    for (const lang of ["lat", "en", "fr"]) {
        const text = value[lang];
        if (text !== undefined && typeof text !== "string")
            issues.push({ path: `${path}.${lang}`, issue: "Must be a string" });
        if (typeof text === "string" && /<\/?[a-z][^>]*>/i.test(text))
            issues.push({ path: `${path}.${lang}`, issue: "HTML markup is not allowed in canonical liturgical data" });
    }
}
function validateMassStep(step, index = 0) {
    const issues = [];
    const path = `steps[${index}]`;
    if (!step || typeof step !== "object")
        return [{ path, issue: "Step must be an object" }];
    if (!step.id || typeof step.id !== "string")
        issues.push({ path: `${path}.id`, issue: "Stable id is required" });
    if (!step.phase || typeof step.phase !== "string")
        issues.push({ path: `${path}.phase`, issue: "Phase is required" });
    if (!step.section || typeof step.section !== "string")
        issues.push({ path: `${path}.section`, issue: "Section is required" });
    if (!step.title || typeof step.title !== "object")
        issues.push({ path: `${path}.title`, issue: "Title object is required" });
    validateText3(step.title, `${path}.title`, issues);
    validateText3(step.subtitle, `${path}.subtitle`, issues);
    validateText3(step.text, `${path}.text`, issues);
    validateText3(step.audibleFragment, `${path}.audibleFragment`, issues);
    if (!VALID_AUDIBILITY.has(step.audibility))
        issues.push({ path: `${path}.audibility`, issue: `Unsupported audibility: ${String(step.audibility)}` });
    if (step.choir) {
        step.choir.forEach((event, eventIndex) => {
            if (!event.id)
                issues.push({ path: `${path}.choir[${eventIndex}].id`, issue: "Chant event id is required" });
            validateText3(event.text, `${path}.choir[${eventIndex}].text`, issues);
        });
    }
    return issues;
}

            ...(choirText ? { text: choirText } : {}),
            continuation: true,
        };
        out.choir = [...(out.choir || []), dynamicEvent];

function filterOrdinarySteps(steps, options, proper) {
    let out = steps;
    if (proper?.hasGloria === false)
        out = out.filter(step => step.id !== "gloria");

},35:function(module,exports,require){
"use strict";
Object.defineProperty(exports, "__esModule", { value: true });
exports.projectStep = projectStep;
exports.projectSequence = projectSequence;
function unavailable(step, slot, actor) {
    return {
        stepId: step.id,
        kind: "proper-unavailable",
        actor,
        properSlot: slot,
        note: "Proper unavailable; the fixed Ordinary remains available.",
    };
}
function choirEvents(step) {
    return (step.choir || []).map(event => {
        if (event.properStatus === "unavailable" || !event.text)
            return unavailable(step, String(event.properSlot || "proper"), "choir");
        return {
            stepId: step.id,
            kind: "choir",
            actor: "choir",
            text: event.text,
            properSlot: event.properSlot,
            continuation: !!event.continuation,
        };
    });
}
function projectStep(step, mode) {
    const events = [];
    const choir = choirEvents(step);
    if (mode === "missal") {
        if (step.properStatus === "unavailable" && step.properSlot)
            events.push(unavailable(step, step.properSlot, step.actor));
        else if (step.text)
            events.push({ stepId: step.id, kind: "altar", actor: step.actor, text: step.text, properSlot: step.properSlot });
        events.push(...choir);
        return { mode, stepId: step.id, events };
    }
    // VOX: choir is always part of what is actually heard.
    events.push(...choir);
    if (step.properStatus === "unavailable" && step.properSlot && step.audibility !== "quiet" && step.audibility !== "silent") {
        events.push(unavailable(step, step.properSlot, step.actor));
        return { mode, stepId: step.id, events };
    }
    if (step.audibility === "audible") {
        if (step.text)
            events.push({ stepId: step.id, kind: "altar", actor: step.actor, text: step.text, properSlot: step.properSlot });
    }
    else if (step.audibility === "mixed") {
        if (step.audibleFragment)
            events.push({ stepId: step.id, kind: "altar", actor: step.actor, text: step.audibleFragment, properSlot: step.properSlot });
        else if (step.text && !step.privateAction)
            events.push({ stepId: step.id, kind: "altar", actor: step.actor, text: step.text, properSlot: step.properSlot });
    }
    else if (step.audibility === "choir") {
        // Choir already emitted above.
    }
    return { mode, stepId: step.id, events };
}
function projectSequence(steps, mode) {
    return steps.map(step => projectStep(step, mode));
}

"use strict";
Object.defineProperty(exports, "__esModule", { value: true });
exports.validateMassStep = validateMassStep;
exports.validateMassSequence = validateMassSequence;
const VALID_AUDIBILITY = new Set(["audible", "quiet", "silent", "mixed", "choir"]);
function validateText3(value, path, issues) {
    if (!value)
        return;
    for (const lang of ["lat", "en", "fr"]) {
        const text = value[lang];
        if (text !== undefined && typeof text !== "string")
            issues.push({ path: `${path}.${lang}`, issue: "Must be a string" });
        if (typeof text === "string" && /<\/?[a-z][^>]*>/i.test(text))
            issues.push({ path: `${path}.${lang}`, issue: "HTML markup is not allowed in canonical liturgical data" });
    }

function unavailable(step, slot, actor) {
    return {
        stepId: step.id,
        kind: "proper-unavailable",
        actor,
        properSlot: slot,
        note: "Proper unavailable; the fixed Ordinary remains available.",
    };
}
function choirEvents(step) {
    return (step.choir || []).map(event => {
        if (event.properStatus === "unavailable" || !event.text)
            return unavailable(step, String(event.properSlot || "proper"), "choir");
        return {
            stepId: step.id,
            kind: "choir",
            actor: "choir",
            text: event.text,
            properSlot: event.properSlot,
            continuation: !!event.continuation,
        };
    });
}
function projectStep(step, mode) {
    const events = [];
    const choir = choirEvents(step);
    if (mode === "missal") {
        if (step.properStatus === "unavailable" && step.properSlot)
            events.push(unavailable(step, step.properSlot, step.actor));
        else if (step.text)
            events.push({ stepId: step.id, kind: "altar", actor: step.actor, text: step.text, properSlot: step.properSlot });
        events.push(...choir);
        return { mode, stepId: step.id, events };
    }
    // VOX: choir is always part of what is actually heard.
    events.push(...choir);
    if (step.properStatus === "unavailable" && step.properSlot && step.audibility !== "quiet" && step.audibility !== "silent") {
        events.push(unavailable(step, step.properSlot, step.actor));
        return { mode, stepId: step.id, events };
    }
    if (step.audibility === "audible") {
        if (step.text)
            events.push({ stepId: step.id, kind: "altar", actor: step.actor, text: step.text, properSlot: step.properSlot });
    }
    else if (step.audibility === "mixed") {
        if (step.audibleFragment)
            events.push({ stepId: step.id, kind: "altar", actor: step.actor, text: step.audibleFragment, properSlot: step.properSlot });
        else if (step.text && !step.privateAction)
            events.push({ stepId: step.id, kind: "altar", actor: step.actor, text: step.text, properSlot: step.properSlot });
    }
    else if (step.audibility === "choir") {
        // Choir already emitted above.
    }
    return { mode, stepId: step.id, events };
}
function projectSequence(steps, mode) {
    return steps.map(step => projectStep(step, mode));
}

(()=>{'use strict';
 const pad=n=>String(n).padStart(2,'0');
 function format(value){
   if(value==null||value==='')return '';
   if(value instanceof Date&&!Number.isNaN(value.getTime()))return `${pad(value.getDate())}/${pad(value.getMonth()+1)}/${value.getFullYear()}`;
   const raw=String(value).trim();
   const iso=raw.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})(?:$|T)/);
   if(iso)return `${iso[3]}/${iso[2]}/${iso[1]}`;
   const d=new Date(value);
   if(!Number.isNaN(d.getTime()))return `${pad(d.getDate())}/${pad(d.getMonth()+1)}/${d.getFullYear()}`;
   return raw;
 }
 globalThis.AO_DISPLAY_DATE=format;
})();

first = specialSectionText(proper, "Benedictio Nuptialis 1");
    const second = specialSectionText(proper, "Benedictio Nuptialis 2");
    const finalBlessing = specialSectionText(proper, "Benedictio Finalis");
    let steps = sequence.steps.slice();
    if (first && second) {
        const pater = steps.findIndex(step => step.id === "pater");
        if (pater >= 0) {
            const inserted = [
                nuptialStep("nuptial-blessing-1",
                    { lat:"Benedictio nuptialis", en:"Nuptial Blessing · First Prayer", fr:"Bénédiction nuptiale · Première oraison" },
                    { en:"After the Pater noster · the spouses kneel before the altar", fr:"Après le Pater noster · les époux s’agenouillent devant l’autel" }, first),
                nuptialStep("nuptial-blessing-2",
                    { lat:"Benedictio nuptialis", en:"Nuptial Blessing · Second Prayer", fr:"Bénédiction nuptiale · Deuxième oraison" },
                    { en:"The celebrant continues the Nuptial Blessing before Libera nos", fr:"Le célébrant poursuit la bénédiction nuptiale avant le Libera nos" }, second)
            ];
            steps = [...steps.slice(0, pater + 1), ...inserted, ...steps.slice(pater + 1)];
        }
    }
    if (finalBlessing) {
        const dismissal = steps.findIndex(step => step.id === "dismissal");
        if (dismissal >= 0) {
            const finalStep = nuptialStep("nuptial-final-blessing",
                { lat:"Benedictio finalis sponsorum", en:"Final Blessing of the Spouses", fr:"Bénédiction finale des époux" },
                { en:"After the dismissal and before Placeat", fr:"Après le renvoi et avant le Placeat" }, finalBlessing);
            finalStep.phase = "Conclusion";
            finalStep.section = "Nuptial Blessing";
            steps = [...steps.slice(0, dismissal + 1), finalStep, ...steps.slice(dismissal + 1)];
        }
    }
    return { ...sequence, steps };
}
function supplementalCollectStep(kind, text) {
    const nuptial = kind === "nuptial";
    return {
        id: nuptial ? "nuptial-collect-commemoration" : "impeded-votive-collect-commemoration",
        phase: "Mass of the Catechumens",
        section: "Collect",
        title: nuptial
            ? { lat:"Oratio pro sponsis", en:"Nuptial Collect", fr:"Collecte nuptiale" }
            : { lat:"Oratio Missae votivae impeditae", en:"Collect of the impeded votive Mass", fr:"Collecte de la Messe votive empêchée" },
        subtitle: nuptial
            ? { en:"Added to the Collect of the Mass of the Day under one conclusion", fr:"Ajoutée à la Collecte de la Messe du jour sous une seule conclusion" }
            : { en:"Retained with the Collect of the Mass of the Day under one conclusion", fr:"Conservée avec la Collecte de la Messe du jour sous une seule conclusion" },
        actor: "celebrant",
        priestPosition: "Epistle side",
        audibility: "audible",
        privateAction: false,
        posture: { value: "STAND", policy: "rubric", source: "resolved-mass-assembly" },
        gestures: [],
        text,
        exceptional: { rite: "resolved-mass-addition", phase: nuptial ? "Nuptial Collect" : "Impeded Votive Collect" },
    };
}
function insertSupplementalCollect(sequence, requestedProper, kind) {
    const text = (requestedProper?.collects || [])[0] || requestedProper?.collect || null;
    if (!text)
        return sequence;
    const steps = sequence.steps.slice();
    const idx = steps.findIndex(step => step.id === "collect");
    if (idx < 0)
        return sequence;
    return { ...sequence, steps: [...steps.slice(0, idx + 1), supplementalCollectStep(kind, text), ...steps.slice(idx + 1)] };
}
function applyResolvedAssembly(sequence, active) {
    const plan = active?.assemblyPlan;
    if (!plan)
        return sequence;
    const requestedProper = active.requestedProper || null;
    let out = sequence;
    if (plan.retainedCollect)
        out = insertSupplementalCollect(out, requestedProper, plan.kind === "nuptial-mass-of-day" ? "nuptial" : "votive");
    if (plan.nuptialBlessings)
        out = insertNuptialRites(out, requestedProper);
    return out;
}

Le Kyrie, le Gloria lorsqu’il est prescrit, le Credo lorsqu’il est prescrit, le Sanctus et l’Agnus appartiennent à l’Ordinaire récurrent ; l’Introït, la Collecte, les lectures, le Graduel ou le Trait, l’Offertoire, la Secrète, la Communion et la Postcommunion dépendent de la célébration.","Le calendrier et les rubriques déterminent quel formulaire est effectivement en vigueur, y compris les commémoraisons et les jours exceptionnels. Faithful Cross, the one noble tree above all others! No forest bears thy equal in leaf, flower or fruit. Sweet wood, sweet nails, bearing so sweet a burden.\n\n℣. Sing, O tongue, the laurels of the glorious contest; proclaim above the trophy of the Cross the noble triumph by which the Redeemer of the world conquered through His sacrifice.\nAnt. Faithful Cross, the one noble tree above all others! No forest bears thy equal in leaf, flower or fruit.\n\n℣. The Creator, grieving over the fall of our first parent when he ate the deadly fruit, then chose a tree to undo the harm wrought by a tree.\nAnt. Sweet wood, sweet nails, bearing so sweet a burden.\n\n℣. The order of our salvation required this work: that art might defeat the many forms of the deceiver's art, and healing might come from the very place where the enemy had wounded.\nAnt. Faithful Cross.\n\n℣. When at last the fullness of sacred time had come, the Son, the Creator of the world, was sent from the Father's throne and came forth clothed in flesh from the Virgin's womb.\nAnt. Sweet wood.\n\n℣. ignoring the congregation.","summary_fr":"L’orientation liturgique commune concerne la direction du culte, non le fait que le prêtre ignorerait l’assemblée.","body":["In the usual arrangement of the traditional Roman Mass, priest and people are oriented together toward the altar, cross and liturgical east. The action is addressed principally to God; the celebrant leads the Church’s prayer rather than conducting a face-to-face meeting with the congregation.","This must not be turned into a false historical absolute. The 1962 Ritus servandus explicitly gives instructions for an altar that is ad orientem, versus populum: if the celebrant already faces the people, he does not turn away from the altar for Dominus vobiscum, Orate fratres, Ite missa est or the blessing. The rubrics therefore know more than one physical church arrangement.","The best way to understand ad orientem is therefore as common liturgical orientation: the priest’s bodily position serves the sacrificial and Godward structure of the rite. Historical claims about the exact orientation of every ancient Roman church should be treated as scholarship, not as a rubric or dogma."],"body_fr":["Dans la disposition habituelle de la Messe romaine traditionnelle, le prêtre et les fidèles sont orientés ensemble vers l’autel, la croix et l’orient liturgique. "use strict";
Object.defineProperty(exports, "__esModule", { value: true });
exports.FormularySelectionStore = void 0;
class FormularySelectionStore {
    values = new Map();
    get(date) { return this.values.get(date) || 0; }
    set(date, index) { this.values.set(date, Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(index) || 0))); }
    clear(date) { if (date)
        this.values.delete(date);
    else
        this.values.clear(); }
    clamp(date, count) {
        if (count <= 0)
            return 0;
        const value = Math.max(0, Math.min(this.get(date), count - 1));
        if (value !== this.get(date))
            this.values.set(date, value);
        return value;
    }
}
exports.FormularySelectionStore = FormularySelectionStore;

class ProperResolver {

    parsedCache = new Map();
    constructor(fetcher) {
        this.fetcher = fetcher;

    clear() { this.parsedCache.clear(); this.fetcher.clear?.(); }
    upstreamUrls(path, language) {
        const directory = source_config_1.LANGUAGE_DIR[language];
        const urls = [`${source_config_1.DIVINUM_MISSA_BASE}/${directory}/${path}.txt`];
        // v14: Missale Meum's normalized 1962 layer references a number of Mass Commons
        // which were later moved out of Divinum Officium's live missa tree. Recover only
        // Commune/* from the same pinned repository revision's historical Mass corpus.
        // This is source recovery, not translation or generated text.
        if (/^Commune\//.test(path)) {
            const legacyDirectory = language === 'fr' ? 'French' : directory;
            const legacyBase = source_config_1.DIVINUM_MISSA_BASE.replace('/web/www/missa', '/obsolete/missa');
            urls.push(`${legacyBase}/${legacyDirectory}/${path}.txt`);
        }
        // v15: the normalized daily Requiem (Votive/Defunctorum) is source-equivalent
        // to the historical Mass-of-the-Dead C9 corpus. Use the same pinned revision

            const legacyDirectory = language === 'fr' ? 'French' : directory;

            urls.push(`${legacyBase}/${legacyDirectory}/Commune/C9.txt`);
        }
        urls.push(`${source_config_1.DIVINUM_HORAS_BASE}/${directory}/${path}.txt`);
        return urls;
    }

function aoWhySourcesForStep(step, enrichment, lang) {
    const ids = [];
    const add = (...values) => values.forEach(v => { if (v && !ids.includes(v)) ids.push(v); });
    add('mr1962','oconnell1964');
    const id = String(step?.id || '');
    const holyWeek = /^(palms-|holy-thursday-|gf-|vigil-)/.test(id);
    if (holyWeek) add('holyweek1955','fortescue1962');
    else if (/^(candlemas-|ashes-)/.test(id)) add('fortescue1962');
    if (AO_THEOLOGY_STEPS.has(id)) add('mediatorDei','ccc');
    if (AO_HISTORY_STEPS.has(id)) add('fortescueMass','jungmann');
    (enrichment?.sources || []).forEach(add);
    return ids.map(k => {
        const base = AO_WHY_SOURCES[k];
        if (!base) return null;
        const locus = aoSourceLocus(id, k, lang);
        return locus ? { ...base, locus } : base;
    }).filter(Boolean);
}

function properText(proper, slot, stepId) {
    if (!proper)
        return undefined;
    switch (slot) {
        case "introit": return (0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.introit);
        case "collects": return (0, ordinary_1.concatText3)([ordinary_1.fixedOrdinary.collectDialogue, ...normaliseList(proper.collects)]);
        case "epistle": return (0, ordinary_1.concatText3)([(0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.epistle), responseDeoGratias]);
        case "preGospelChants": {
            const chants = (proper.preGospelChants || []).filter(Boolean);
            if (!chants.length)
                return (0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.gradual);
            const exact = chants.find(ch => (ch.id || ch.kind || "").toLowerCase() === stepId.toLowerCase());
            const stepKind = stepId.toLowerCase();
            const semantic = chants.find(ch => (ch.kind || "").toLowerCase().includes(stepKind));
            const byIndex = stepKind === "gradual" ? chants[0] : stepKind === "alleluia" ? chants.find(ch => /allel/i.test(ch.kind || "")) || chants[1] : undefined;
            return (0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)((exact || semantic || byIndex || chants[chants.length - 1])?.text);
        }
        case "sequence": return (0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.sequence);
        case "gospel": return (0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.gospel);
        case "offertory": return (0, ordinary_1.concatText3)([ordinary_1.fixedOrdinary.offertoryDialogue, (0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.offertory)]);
        case "secrets": return (0, ordinary_1.concatText3)(normaliseList(proper.secrets));
        case "preface": return (0, ordinary_1.concatText3)([ordinary_1.fixedOrdinary.prefaceDialogue, (0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.preface)]);
        case "communion": return (0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.communion);
        case "postcommunions": return (0, ordinary_1.concatText3)([ordinary_1.fixedOrdinary.postcommunionDialogue, ...normaliseList(proper.postcommunions)]);
        case "superPopulum": return (0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.superPopulum);
        default: return undefined;
    }
}
function properChoirText(proper, slot, stepId) {
    if (!proper)
        return undefined;
    if (slot === "preGospelChants") {
        const chants = (proper.preGospelChants || []);
        if (!chants.length)

function attachProper(step, proper, properAvailable) {
    const out = clone(step);
    if (out.id === "gospel-announcement") {
        out.text = gospelAnnouncementText(proper);
        if (Array.isArray(out.gestures) && out.gestures[0]) {
            out.gestures[0].anchorLat = 'Sequéntia ✠ sancti Evangélii';
            out.gestures[0].anchorEng = 'Continuation ✠ of the Holy Gospel';
        }
    }
    if (out.properSlot) {
        const sourceReady = properAvailable && properSlotHasSource(proper, out.properSlot);
        const resolved = sourceReady ? properText(proper, out.properSlot, out.id) : undefined;
        out.properStatus = hasText(resolved) ? "ready" : "unavailable";
        if (resolved)
            out.text = resolved;
        else
            delete out.text;
        if (out.id === "secret")
            out.audibleFragment = secretConclusion;
    }
    if (out.concurrentProperChoir) {
        const choirSourceReady = properAvailable && properSlotHasSource(proper, out.concurrentProperChoir);
        const choirText = choirSourceReady ? properChoirText(proper, out.concurrentProperChoir, out.id) : undefined;
        const dynamicEvent = {
            id: `${out.id}-${out.concurrentProperChoir}-choir`,
            role: "choir",
            properSlot: out.concurrentProperChoir,
            properStatus: hasText(choirText) ? "ready" : "unavailable",
            ...(choirText ? { text: choirText } : {}),
            continuation: true,
        };
        out.choir = [...(out.choir || []), dynamicEvent];
    }
    delete out.concurrentProperChoir;
    return out;
}

function filterOrdinarySteps(steps, options, proper) {
    let out = steps;
    if (proper?.hasGloria === false)
        out = out.filter(step => step.id !== "gloria");
    if (proper?.hasCredo === false)
        out = out.filter(step => step.id !== "credo");
    if (options.form === "sung" && options.incenseSung === false) {
        out = out.filter(step => step.id !== "incense-entrance" && step.id !== "incense-off");
    }
    if (options.omitJudica || proper?.isRequiem)
        out = out.filter(step => step.id !== "judica");
    if (options.faithfulCommunion === false) {
        out = out.filter(step => !["second-confiteor", "ecce", "domine-non-sum-dignus", "communion-faithful"].includes(step.id));
    }
    else if (options.secondConfiteor) {
        const idx = out.findIndex(step => step.id === "ecce");
        if (idx >= 0)
            out = [...out.slice(0, idx), makeSecondConfiteor(!!options.joinConfiteor), ...out.slice(idx)];
    }
    // 1962 SOT: at Sung Mass, if the faithful receive, the Communion chant begins
    // with distribution after the communicants' triple Domine non sum dignus.
    // If nobody receives, it begins during the priest's Communion.
    if (options.form === "sung" && options.faithfulCommunion !== false) {
        out = out.map(step => {
            if (step.id !== "priest-communion" || !step.concurrentProperChoir)
                return step;
            const copy = { ...step };
            delete copy.concurrentProperChoir;
            return copy;
        });
    }
    if (proper?.omitLastGospel || proper?.followedByLiturgicalAction) {
        out = out.filter(step => step.id !== "last-gospel-announcement" && step.id !== "last-gospel");
    }
    return out;
}
function insertSuperPopulum(steps, proper, properAvailable) {
    const text = properAvailable ? (0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper?.superPopulum) : undefined;
    if (!text)
        return steps;
    const idx = steps.findIndex(step => step.id === "postcommunion");
    if (idx < 0)
        return steps;
    const step = {
        id: "super-populum",
        phase: "Conclusion",
        section: "Postcommunion",
        title: { lat: "Oratio super populum", en: "Prayer over the People", fr: "Oraison sur le peuple" },
        actor: "celebrant",
        audibility: "audible",
        privateAction: false,
        text,
        properSlot: "superPopulum",
        properStatus: "ready",
    };
    return [...steps.slice(0, idx + 1), step, ...steps.slice(idx + 1)];
}

function insertSermonPause(steps) {
    const idx = steps.findIndex(step => step.id === "gospel");
    if (idx < 0 || steps.some(step => step.id === "sermon-pause"))
        return steps;
    const sermon = {
        id: "sermon-pause",
        phase: "Mass of the Catechumens",
        section: "Gospel & Creed",
        title: { en: "Sermon / Homily · if present", fr: "Sermon / Homélie · si présent" },
        subtitle: { en: "If preaching occurs, stay here until it ends. If not, continue.", fr: "S’il y a une prédication, restez ici jusqu’à sa fin. Sinon, continuez." },
        actor: "preacher",
        priestPosition: "Preaching place",
        audibility: "audible",
        privateAction: false,
        posture: { value: "CUSTOM", policy: "local", source: "sermon" },
        gestures: [],
        explanation: "The sermon is a navigation pause, not a prayer-text step of the Ordo Missae. Do not advance the Mass timeline because of elapsed preaching time."
    };
    return [...steps.slice(0, idx + 1), sermon, ...steps.slice(idx + 1)];
}

    }
    // 1962 SOT: at Sung Mass, if the faithful receive, the Communion chant begins
    // with distribution after the communicants' triple Domine non sum dignus.
    // If nobody receives, it begins during the priest's Communion.

            return copy;
        });

        out = out.filter(step => step.id !== "last-gospel-announcement" && step.id !== "last-gospel");
    }
    return out;

function prayerHtml(v){return nl(v).replace(/ (V\.) /g,'<br><br>$1 ').replace(/ (R\.) /g,'<br>$1 ').replace(/ (℣\.) /g,'<br><br>$1 ').replace(/ (℟\.) /g,'<br>$1 ').replace(/ (Oremus\.) /g,'<br><br>$1 ').replace(/ (Let us pray\.) /g,'<br><br>$1 ').replace(/ (Prions\.) /g,'<br><br>$1 ');}

function text3(v){if(!v)return {lat:'',en:'',fr:''};if(typeof v==='string')return {lat:v,en:'',fr:''};return {lat:String(v.lat||''),en:String(v.en||''),fr:String(v.fr||'')};}
function join3(list){const rows=(list||[]).map(text3);return {lat:rows.map(x=>x.lat).filter(Boolean).join('\n\n'),en:rows.map(x=>x.en).filter(Boolean).join('\n\n'),fr:rows.map(x=>x.fr).filter(Boolean).join('\n\n')};}
